# Skills · T · part 137

839 entries, transcription-factor-pathway-interaction-analysis to transit-depth-extraction. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **transcription-factor-pathway-interaction-analysis**: Use when analyzing time-series gene expression profiles (e.g., RNA-seq across a biological process such as liver regeneration) to identify which TFs and pathways are activated or repressed at specific time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-pathway-interaction-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-peak-calling**: Use when ChIP-seq FASTQ data for a DNA-binding factor (e.g., LuxR or H-NS in V. harveyi) needs trimming, reference alignment, and macs2 peak calling to count and compare binding peaks across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-peak-calling))
- **transcription-factor-perturbation-analysis**: Use when you have a trained PRESCIENT model on time-series scRNA-seq data and need to test whether perturbing candidate TFs (e.g. neutrophil or monocyte regulators) shifts simulated cells toward or away from expected terminal fates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-perturbation-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-rank-benchmarking**: Use when comparing TR-identification tools on query region sets where the true perturbed/active TR is known, and a rank-based metric (MRR) over ranked TR output is needed for fair method comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-rank-benchmarking))
- **transcription-factor-regulatory-network-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic single-cell expression count matrices with known branched trajectory trees — e.g. to benchmark trajectory or tree-alignment methods where ground-truth topology and pseudotime are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-regulatory-network-simulation))
- **transcription-factor-regulatory-potential-computation**: Use when you have four single-cell datasets (one scRNA-seq + one scATAC-seq from each of two populations) with consistent cluster assignments, and you need to detect subpopulation-specific differential TF-target regulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-regulatory-potential-computation))
- **transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether inferred gene regulatory network edges are biologically meaningful — e.g., testing if a TF's neighbors in a cell-type-specific network match its ENCODE-validated targets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis--0f53fff212970a26))
- **transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a list of predicted circRNA/RBP candidate regulators and a gold-standard regulator list, and need to test shared transcription factor modulation (or related Harmonizome associations) to validate the candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis--0fc3b562f1e2c623))
- **transcription-factor-target-gene-identification**: Use when you have a TF ChIP-seq peak file (e.g. an ENCODE BED file) and need the set of unique genes whose associated REMs overlap the TF's binding regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-gene-identification))
- **transcription-factor-target-identification**: Use when the goal is to identify candidate transcription factor targets by correlating gene expression across many diverse GEO experiments, given a mean-centered COnTORT output file with GenBank annotation columns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-identification))
- **transcription-factor-weight-bounded-regression**: Use when modeling a target gene's RNA dynamics as a weighted function of candidate TF expressions (from ENCODE/ChEA) and you need bounded, solvable TF weight estimates during EM-based gene dynamics fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-weight-bounded-regression))
- **transcription-factor-weight-ranking**: Use when TFvelo has been run on a scRNA-seq dataset (rc.h5ad with TF weight matrix) and you need to identify the top-weighted regulators or verify TF weight signs on pathway genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-weight-ranking))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-strand-asymmetry-estimation))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-strand-bias-testing))
- **transcriptional-subtype-classification-transfer**: Use when a new bulk RNA-Seq cohort needs subtype labels propagated from an already-annotated reference cohort, especially when cohorts share no samples and only partially overlapping genes. - **transcriptome-chimera-identification-blastx**: Use when you have a de novo assembled transcriptome (e.g. Trinity FASTA) with no reference genome and need to count or mark chimeric transcripts to assess or benchmark assembly filtering. - **transcriptome-clustering-comparison**: Use when you need to quantify whether parallelizing RCC's consensus clustering across multiple CCP processes changes clustering accuracy (ARI) or processing time relative to a single-process run with more repeats. - **transcriptome-evolutionary-index-calculation**: Use when single-cell RNA data (Scanpy AnnData) needs a gene age-weighted expression index per cell, stage, or cell type — e.g. to detect whether young gene age classes peak in early embryos — or when per-class partial TEI / relative expression profiles are required. - **transcriptome-metabolome-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have paired spatial transcriptome and metabolome feature matrices (e.g., from . - **transfer-rumor-check**: 查证足球转会传闻的可信度。当用户提到一个转会说法——哪怕只是模糊一句"听说X要去Y队""刷到Z要转会了""这个转会靠谱吗"——想知道靠不靠谱/是真是假/查证一下时使用。沿两个正交维度判断——真实性（这个转会说法有多可能是真的，六档）+ 时效性（它现在还成不成立，含联网核实转会最新状态）——分开呈现，绝不合成一个分。核心方法：**信源是系统主动去联网检索的，不是等用户提供**——用户给的（球员+球队+方向）是检索线索，skill 拿它去查"谁在报这事、最可靠的源说到哪一步"，用检索到的报道场判可信度。信源当"证据"用（用"谁说的"推断"有多可能真"），不给媒体本身打分/做画像。不要触发：只是聊某笔转会的看法、问球员数据/身价、讨论战术——那些不是查证传闻。仅处理纯文字输入（不接链接抓取/截图识图）。国际传闻走机构为主的分级；中超/中甲/中国球员传闻走另一套中文一手源分级。 ([Wolinaxia/transfer-rumor-check](https://github.com/Wolinaxia/transfer-rumor-check/tree/HEAD/claude/transfer-rumor-check) · ★7)

Run before merging any PR that touches transit_agent/, transit_auth/, deny.toml, or Cargo.toml. The handoff separates supplied facts, inferences and unknowns, and unknowns stay marked on the map. Use when someone asks for an architecture diagram, system map, data-flow map or process map they can explore, or asks for a transit or subway style diagram.
